- **inkify**: Write a Chinese character (or any stroke-ordered glyph) on a web page in real brush-and-ink, stroke by stroke, at a human hand's rhythm. Offline it renders each stroke with a brush of 72 bristles on rice paper (飞白 dry-brush streaks, ink pooling where the brush rests, 洇墨 bleed along paper fibers) into one texture atlas; the page then writes it with pure SVG + CSS animations that start on first paint, no JavaScript needed. Use for calligraphy intros and hero animations, name or logo reveals, 毛笔书写动画, 书法开场, 水墨字, 写字动画, or to turn a flat Hanzi Writer / stroke-dashoffset vector wipe into ink.
